# Register Sumber

Setiap fakta eksternal di dokumen proyek merujuk ke salah satu entri di sini dengan label `[SRC-xx]`. Aturan lengkap: `.claude/skills/runbook/references/anti-halu.md`.

Sebuah entri hanya boleh ditambahkan setelah halamannya **benar-benar dibuka**, bukan berdasarkan cuplikan hasil pencarian.

| SRC-265 | OWASP ASVS 5.0.0, bab V6 Authentication (teks requirement, tag v5.0.0 di repositori resmi) | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/OWASP/ASVS/v5.0.0/5.0/en/0x15-V6-Authentication.md | 2 | 2026-11-18 | 6.2.1 (L1) "Verify that user set passwords are at least 8 characters in length", "a minimum of 15 characters is strongly recommended"; 6.2.6 (L1) isian bertipe password, boleh ditampilkan sementara; 6.2.7 (L1) "Verify that "paste" functionality, browser password helpers, and external password managers are permitted."; 6.2.8 (L1) "exactly as received from the user, without any modifications such as truncation or case transformation."; 6.2.9 (L2) "Verify that passwords of at least 64 characters are permitted."; 6.2.10 (L2) "The application must not require periodic credential rotation."; 6.2.11 (L2) "the documented list of context specific words is used to prevent easy to guess passwords being created"; 6.2.12 (L2) "passwords submitted during account registration or password changes are checked against a set of breached passwords"; 6.1.1 dokumentasi kontrol "rate limiting, anti-automation, and adaptive response"; 6.3.1 (L1) kontrol terhadap credential stuffing dan brute force diterapkan sesuai dokumentasi; 6.3.3 (L2) "either a multi-factor authentication mechanism or a combination of single-factor authentication mechanisms"; pengantar V6.2: "From L2 onwards, multi-factor authentication mechanisms are required, where passwords may be one of those factors."; 6.3.5 (L3) pemberitahuan upaya autentikasi mencurigakan; 6.3.7 (L3) pemberitahuan setelah perubahan detail autentikasi; 6.3.8 (L3) "Verify that valid users cannot be deduced from failed authentication challenges", berlaku juga untuk pendaftaran dan lupa password; 6.4.3 (L2) atur ulang password tidak melewati MFA. Bab tidak memuat requirement khusus batas laju email pendaftaran/atur ulang. Dibuka ulang 2026-11-18 oleh security-architect (`docs/review/tahap-07-security.md` §8 R-1): pengantar V6.3 "L2 applications must force the use of multi-factor authentication (MFA)."; 6.3.3 (L2) "...must be used in order to access the application." dan "Relaxing any of the considerations in this requirement requires a fully documented rationale and a comprehensive set of mitigating controls."; 6.2.2 (L1) "Verify that users can change their password."; 6.2.3 (L1) "...requires the user's current and new password"; 6.3.4 (L2) "there are no undocumented pathways"; 6.1.3 (L2) jalur autentikasi terdokumentasi; 6.4.3 (L2) "...does not bypass any enabled multi-factor authentication mechanisms."; 6.4.4 (L2) pembuktian identitas saat faktor hilang; pengantar V6.6 "Unsafe out-of-band authentication mechanisms such as e-mail and VOIP are not permitted."; 6.6.1 (L2) PSTN bersyarat. | SRC-290 | Node.js: berkas LICENSE pada tag v26.13.1 (repositori resmi nodejs/node) | https://raw.githubusercontent.com/nodejs/node/v26.13.1/LICENSE | 2 | 2026-11-18 | "Node.js is licensed for use as follows:"; "Copyright Node.js contributors. All rights reserved."; "Permission is hereby granted, free of charge, to any person obtaining a copy of this software and associated documentation files (the "Software"), to deal in the Software without restriction, ..." (teks izin, syarat pencantuman, dan penafian "AS IS" berbentuk MIT, tetapi bagian Node.js tidak menyebut nama "MIT"). Berkas juga memuat lisensi komponen pihak ketiga ("This license applies to parts of Node.js originating from the https://github.com/joyent/node repository:"; komponen pertama yang dicantumkan antara lain Acorn, c-ares, merve, ittapi, amaro, dengan lisensi masing-masing). Hanya 100.000 karakter pertama dari ±156.000 yang dibaca; daftar lengkap lisensi komponen pihak ketiga tidak diperiksa. Menjawab stack §13.2 butir 19 (Node.js) | 05-stack.md §1.2 (lisensi runtime), F1-T04 |

| SRC-387 | Kunci konfigurasi clippy untuk larangan (allow-*-in-tests, allow-dbg-in-tests, disallowed-methods dengan path/reason/allow-invalid) dan lint unwrap_used, expect_used, panic, todo, unimplemented, indexing_slicing, dbg_macro, disallowed_methods | https://doc.rust-lang.org/clippy/lint_configuration.html; cargo clippy 1.100.0 dijalankan (F1-T12) | 1 | 2026-11-19 | Halaman konfigurasi memuat allow-unwrap-in-tests, allow-expect-in-tests, allow-panic-in-tests, allow-indexing-slicing-in-tests, allow-dbg-in-tests, disallowed-methods (path wajib; reason, replacement, allow-invalid opsional). Dijalankan: satu clippy.toml di root workspace berlaku untuk semua crate (clippy.toml per crate dihapus), `[workspace.lints.clippy]` + `[lints] workspace = true` diterima; 14 pelanggaran uji (unwrap, expect, panic, todo, unimplemented, indexing, dbg, now_utc, now_local, UtcDateTime::now, SystemTime::now, env::var/var_os/vars/vars_os) semuanya menggagalkan `cargo clippy -- -D warnings`, kontrol tanpa pelanggaran lulus. `allow-*-in-tests` hanya mencakup fungsi `#[test]`/`#[cfg(test)]`, bukan fungsi pembantu di `tests/*.rs` (tetap perlu `#![allow(clippy::unwrap_used, ...)]` di berkas test, pola yang sudah ada) | clippy.toml, Cargo.toml (workspace.lints), crates/arch-check; menyelesaikan butir coding-standards §3.4 dan §5.7 untuk seluruh workspace |

| SRC-530 | jsonwebtoken 12.0.0: crates.io API (fitur, dependensi) + source crate (`src/decoding.rs`, `src/validation.rs`, `src/header.rs`, `src/algorithms.rs`, `src/jwk.rs`, `src/errors.rs`, `src/crypto/mod.rs`, `src/crypto/aws_lc/{mod,rsa}.rs`) | https://crates.io/api/v1/crates/jsonwebtoken/12.0.0 ; https://static.crates.io/crates/jsonwebtoken/jsonwebtoken-12.0.0.crate | 2 | 2026-11-20 | Fitur: `default = ["use_pem"]`, `aws_lc_rs = ["dep:aws-lc-rs"]` (aws-lc-rs ^1.15.0 dengan fitur bawaannya), `rust_crypto`; tanpa `use_pem` tidak ada pem/simple_asn1. `decode(token, &DecodingKey, &Validation)`: menolak bila `alg` header tidak ada di `validation.algorithms`, lalu bila keluarga verifier berbeda dari keluarga setiap algoritma yang diizinkan (`InvalidAlgorithm`), memverifikasi tanda tangan (`InvalidSignature`), lalu `validate`. `Validation`: `set_issuer`, `set_audience`, `set_required_spec_claims` (hanya "exp","nbf","aud","iss","sub"), `validate_exp`/`validate_nbf` memakai `SystemTime::now` di dalam pustaka, `validate_aud` bawaan true dan `aud` hadir tanpa audience terkonfigurasi = `InvalidAudience`. `Algorithm` tidak punya varian `none` (`FromStr` gagal → `InvalidAlgorithmName`). `DecodingKey::from_rsa_components(n, e)` (base64url), `from_ec_components(x, y)`. Verifier RSA aws-lc: `RSA_PKCS1_2048_8192_SHA*`/`RSA_PSS_2048_8192_SHA*` (kunci < 2048 bit ditolak); verifier HMAC menolak kunci keluarga lain. Header token akses `{alg: RS256, typ: JWT, kid}`; klaim token akses memuat `iss` = `<url dasar>/realms/<realm>`, `aud`, `typ: "Bearer"`, `azp`, `sid` (string non-UUID), `exp`, `iat`, `jti`, `scope`; token akses `admin-cli` master **tanpa** `sub`. ID token `typ: "ID"`, `aud` = client id; refresh token `typ: "Refresh"`, header `alg: HS512`. Konfigurasi realm proyek (audience, klaim `sub`) belum ada (F1-T31) | crates/authn (F1-T32) |

| SRC-582 | rustls 0.23.46, tokio-rustls 0.26.6, rustls-pki-types 1.16.0, rustls-webpki 0.103.17 (+ subtle 2.6.1, untrusted 0.9.0) di crates.io API + kode sumber registry lokal | https://crates.io/api/v1/crates/rustls/0.23.46 ; https://crates.io/api/v1/crates/tokio-rustls/0.26.6 ; https://crates.io/api/v1/crates/rustls-pki-types/1.16.0 ; https://crates.io/api/v1/crates/rustls-webpki/0.103.17 | 2 | 2026-11-20 | Lisensi: rustls "Apache-2.0 OR ISC OR MIT"; tokio-rustls dan rustls-pki-types "MIT OR Apache-2.0"; rustls-webpki "ISC"; subtle BSD-3-Clause; untrusted ISC (semua di daftar izin deny.toml). Tidak di-yank; rustls 0.23.46 dibuat 2026-10-14 (MSRV 1.71), tokio-rustls 0.26.6 2026-09-28, rustls-pki-types 1.16.0 2026-11-10, rustls-webpki 0.103.17 2026-11-12; repositori github.com/rustls. Fitur rustls `aws_lc_rs` memakai `aws-lc-rs ^1.18` (diunifikasi ke 1.19.0 yang dipin crate crypto); `ring` dan `logging` tidak dinyalakan. API yang dipakai (source): `ServerConfig/ClientConfig::builder_with_provider`, `with_safe_default_protocol_versions` (TLS 1.2 dan 1.3), `with_no_client_auth`, `with_single_cert`, `with_root_certificates`, `RootCertStore::add_parsable_certificates`, `crypto::aws_lc_rs::default_provider`, `pem::PemObject::{pem_file_iter, from_pem_file}` (fitur `std`), `tokio_rustls::{TlsAcceptor::accept, TlsConnector::connect}`. `ServerConfig::builder()` memakai `get_default_or_install_from_crate_features()` yang memanggil `.expect(...)` bila penyedia tidak dapat ditentukan, sehingga proyek memakai `builder_with_provider(crypto::tls_crypto_provider())` | crates/crypto, crates/ses, crates/email-relay (F1-T36) |

| SRC-584 | Uji F1-T36: relay SMTP dan adaptor SES terhadap tiruan SES HTTPS + OpenBao 2.7.2 dev (dijalankan) | `cargo test -p email-relay` (unit 18, `tests/relay_smtp.rs` 14, `tests/relay_logs.rs` 1); `cargo test -p ses` (unit 9, `tests/ses_credentials_openbao.rs` 1); juga lewat skrip verifikasi repo | 1 | 2026-11-20 | Semua lulus. Klien SMTP + STARTTLS (CA uji `openssl` saat test) → relay → SesClient (rustls, SigV4) → tiruan SES yang memverifikasi SigV4 secara independen. Negatif lulus: MAIL/RCPT/DATA tanpa STARTTLS 530; STARTTLS berbarengan perintah lain ditutup tanpa TLS; `From` di luar daftar izin 550; RCPT kedua 452 (SES menerima satu penerima); `X-SES-*`, `From` lain/ganda, To/Cc/Bcc tambahan, header non-ASCII 554 tanpa panggilan SES; pesan > batas 552; LF/CR tunggal dan baris > 1000 oktet 500 lalu sesi ditutup; SES 429/500/503 dan batas waktu → 451, SES 400 → 554, tanda tangan salah ditolak tiruan (403) → 451; batas per penerima → 250 dan dibuang; batas global → 451; batas koneksi → 421; klien tanpa CA relay gagal TLS; balasan dan log tanpa alamat/subjek/token, log memuat `MessageId` SES | crates/email-relay, crates/ses (F1-T36) |
| SRC-585 | Uji F1-T36: Keycloak 26.8.2 (realm pasien dan admin apa adanya) → relay → tiruan SES (dijalankan) | `bash scripts/keycloak-check.sh ephemeral rinci` (kontainer Keycloak, OpenBao dev, `SesTiruan.java`, relay di namespace jaringan Keycloak) | 1 | 2026-11-20 | 192 lulus, 0 gagal. Keycloak mengirim lewat STARTTLS ke `email-relay` dengan CA uji di `conf/truststores` dan hostname verifier `DEFAULT`; permintaan SES berisi `FromEmailAddress` daftar izin, `Destination` dari RCPT, configuration set, Tracking `DISABLED`. SES 503/429/400 → Admin REST `send-verify-email` HTTP 500 tanpa email; STARTTLS dimatikan di realm atau `from` di luar daftar izin → HTTP 500. "Lupa password" saat SES 503: halaman sama dengan saat sukses, event `SEND_RESET_PASSWORD_ERROR` galat `email_send_failed` tercatat; kunci detail event: `auth_method`, `auth_type`, `code_id`, `reason`, `redirect_uri`, `username`; `reason` = "Error when attempting to send the email to the server. More information is available in the server log." (tanpa alamat/balasan relay), tetapi `username` = alamat email (email sebagai username). Required action Verify Email saat login ketika SES gagal: Keycloak menjawab HTTP 500 (bukan halaman verifikasi), tanpa kode otorisasi. Log relay dan Keycloak tanpa alamat dan token aksi. Batas per penerima harness dinaikkan (1000/jam) karena satu user uji menerima banyak email; pada percobaan pertama dengan batas bawaan 5/jam, pesan ke-6 dibalas 250 dan dibuang sehingga event `email_send_failed` tidak muncul (perilaku ADR-026 butir 4) | skrip harness Keycloak (scripts) dan berkas uji di infra (F1-T36) |
